The check's own docstring says it exists because "the whole failure mode is a true line nobody read"

THIS INSTANCE SHOWS THE RECIPROCAL IS ALSO TRUE: rc IS NOT A RELIABLE SUCCESS SIGNAL EITHER.** A runner keying on **stdout** would have called it success — correct here, by luck. each of its five commits confirmed present on the remote. ★ **So condition ⑧ must be written as a POSITIVE requirement, not a prohibition — *verify at the remote ref*, never merely *do not read stdout*.** A prohibition on one bad signal silently blesses the other bad signal, which is `[[finding_impeachment_must_be_scoped_to_the_claim_not_the_source]]` in the construction direction. ⚠️ PROME's own `safe-push` run the same hour returned the receipt cleanly, so **this divergence is INTERMITTENT and load-dependent — it will not reproduce on a quiet box, and a test written on a quiet OTHER TWO — HENRY 2026-09-14 16:07: *"the receipt named a different HEAD only because another session pushed concurrently — `ac548dfab` landed."*** ⇒ **THE RECEIPT'S NAMED HEAD IS NOT NECESSARILY YOUR HEAD.** With seven sessions on one box, a concurrent push can advance `origin/master` between your push and the receipt's fresh fetch, so the CONFIRMED line truthfully names a head that is **not the one you pushed**. ⛔ **A desk checking *"does the receipt's HEAD equal MY HEAD?"* CONCLUDES FAILURE ON A SUCCESSFUL PUSH** — and that is a THIRD wrong check, after stdout-only (mode 1) and rc-only (mode 2). ★ **ALL THREE MODES OBSERVED WITHIN ONE HOUR ON ONE BOX, EACH BY A DIFFERENT DESK, AND EACH IS A DIFFERENT SIGNAL BEING WRONG: FALCON got receipt-CONFIRMED with rc=1 · PROME got a clean receipt · HENRY got a receipt naming another session's head.** 🔑 **THEREFORE THE CHECK IS NOT ABOUT THE REF AT ALL — IT IS ABOUT MEMBERSHIP: *are MY commits present on `origin/master`?*** Both desks that verified correctly did exactly this and neither read a ref equality: FALCON confirmed its five commits present, HENRY confirmed its six. ⇒ **CONDITION ⑧ REWRITE, superseding the earlier formulation on this row: after a push, verify by fresh fetch that EACH COMMIT THIS SESSION AUTHORED is an ancestor of `origin/master`. Never rc, never stdout, never HEAD equality — all three are wrong in a DIFFERENT direction and only the third is wrong in the direction that manufactures a false FAILURE.** ⚠️ A false failure is the expensive one here: it sends a desk into non-ff recovery it does not need, and `--autostash` on a shared tree with six live sessions is the documented way to sweep another desk's work. ‖ ⛔ **COUNT CORRECTED 2026-09-14 16:2x (ARGUS ❌8): the headline read SEVEN while the body states an ⑧ and rewrites it TWICE — and `HANDOFF` instructs *"verify MEMBERSHIP (L378 ⑧)"*, pointing at a condition the headline said did not exist.** ★ **A row whose stated purpose is to BE the test list under-counted its own tests.** ⑧ is hereby PROMOTED into the numbered list, not left as an addendum: **⑧ AFTER A PUSH, VERIFY BY FRESH FETCH THAT EACH COMMIT THIS SESSION AUTHORED IS AN ANCESTOR OF `origin/master`. CALIBRATE contract_probe STALE_S — the 900-second constant is uncalibrated and load-bearing when the MATCHED candidate is stale. How to read this page in 20 seconds

Pending Will = the action is on you. Needs attention = the action is on the fleet (worst first). Fire ledger = the tripwires armed on your behalf — every one ends at your desk if it trips. Catalyst runway = what the calendar forces. Fleet grid = who's healthy, who's drifting. Everything else is context.

"Gated" vs. a heat color — two different things

A gate is a specific, pre-registered tripwire: a measured threshold + a fixed action + a state. Example — GATE-RESHAPE-BC: condition "HY OAS >280 sustained OR WAL prints 7/21 AMC" → action "the bank-put reshape proposal comes to you." It's written down before the fact so it can't be rationalized after.

To call a risk "gated" means we've wired that plumbing — a defined trigger and a defined response — so a live event produces a clean proposal at your desk instead of a scramble. (Kharg was an open hole on Fri 7/18; gated by Sat — same risk, now with a tripwire and a fire path.)

A heat color (green/yellow/orange/red) answers a different question: how hot is this right now. A risk can be red-hot with no gate, or calm-yellow with a gate armed underneath it. Gates watch; colors describe. And a gate firing never executes a trade — it hands you a proposal.

owner: PROME/GATES.tsv (the fire-ledger canon)

The fire ledger & gate states

A central register of every pre-registered "if X happens, someone must DO something" rule — not predictions that merely get graded, only rules that owe an action (build a packet, arm a trade card, bring you a proposal). PROME scans it at every boot.

Born 2026-07-09 from a real failure: two VIOLET triggers fired on 7/2 into a frozen session and the owed hedge-packet sat orphaned for seven days before an audit found it. The ledger makes that structurally impossible — the watching is centralized even though the trigger logic stays with the domain agents.

LIVEarmed tripwire — condition watched, not yet met
FIRED-UNEXECUTEDcondition came TRUE and the owed action hasn't happened — blocks all new PROME work until cleared or escalated to you. Should never survive a session.
RESOLVEDcondition tested, verdict landed, consequence executed (or explicitly not owed)
LAPSEDfired but the action window passed — your call, recorded, not retro-built

owner: PROME/GATES.tsv · full trigger logic stays in each owner's KB

Arm-#1 / #2 / #3

The three independent arming paths for TERRY's duration/TLT-put fire card (TRY-FIRE-004). Each is a different way the rates thesis can prove itself: #1 was an auction-stress test (resolved NOT-FIRED 7/9), #2 is five consecutive 10Y closes ≥4.50, #3 is foreign official selling in the TIC data. Any single arm completing → TERRY arms the card → comes to you for [Approve] with the live broker book. "Armed" never means "traded" — capital moves only on your explicit approval.

owner: AGENTS/TERRY/setups/FLOW-TRIGGER_duration-TLT-put.md

A card's lifecycle: ARMED → FIRED → FILLED

Three distinct stages — the words are not interchangeable, and only the last one moves capital.

ARMEDsetup conditions met + the trade fully built (strikes, ladder, $500 cap) — loaded, safety on. Never means "traded."
FIREDthe trigger condition crossed — the card now owes you a proposal (TERRY live-re-marks the strikes against the current book)
FILLEDyou gave [Approve] against a live broker book and it executed. You are the trigger, not the tape — nothing reaches here on its own.

Worked example (July 2026, historical): TRY-FIRE-004's arm fired 7/13 → card ARMED. Will first ruled NO-ADD (7/16 — the book already owned the grind), then approved a $330 re-fire on a clean red-day entry (7/20). Armed ≠ auto-fill in either direction — the fill returns as a TERRY proposal, and the current card state lives in GATES/FORGE, never in this example.

owner: AGENTS/TERRY/setups/ (fire cards) · PROME/GATES.tsv (gate rows)

Break / Grind / Unresolved

NEXUS's regime probability split over the next 2–6 weeks — the one numeric regime call in the system, deliberately owned by exactly one agent (PROME runs no competing split, so you always know whose judgment you're reading).

Break — the accumulated stress (rates-vol, credit fuel, record equity complacency) cracks into an actual market repricing. Grind — the calm holds and the stress dissipates or stays latent. Unresolved — the honest bucket: the window ends and the tape still hasn't told us. A high Unresolved number means the decisive tests are still ahead — watch the catalyst runway, not the noise.

owner: AGENTS/NEXUS/STATUS.md (re-anchored after regime-moving events)

Status colors (fleet-wide key)

greennone / healthy / fresh
yellowmonitoring — armed or aging, no action owed yet
orangeelevated — deserves a look this session
redactive/critical — blocking or cold; act or escalate

Same key everywhere in the repo (STATUS files, HEARTBEAT, this page). Color is never the only signal — every chip carries its words.

Needs attention — how it's composed

Auto-assembled at build time, worst first, from: fired-unexecuted gates · failing health checks · live gates unchecked >5d · HEARTBEAT blocking rows · active agents cold (>14d) or lagging (8–14d) · inboxes ≥8 deep · a stale spine audit (>7d). Nothing is hand-curated; if it's listed, a rule put it there. If this rail is ever long every day, the thresholds need tuning — tell PROME.

Fleet grid columns

Maturity (L1–L5, DAEDALUS's scale): L1 scaffold just built → L2 first own data pulls → L3 predictions resolving on a clock → L4 other agents consume its output by name → L5 fully self-running periphery. Last activity = business days ("bd") since any commit touched the agent's directory (≤3 fresh · ≤7 aging · 8–14 lagging · >14 cold — weekends don't age anyone). 30d commits = volume of recent work. Inbox = unprocessed items waiting (excludes processed/). newborn = built within days, no track record yet — low bars are honest, not alarming. parked → date = quiet by plan until that date (expiry-dated register, PROME/tools/ dashboard_parked.tsv); staleness flagging resumes automatically past the date, and inbox flags still apply while parked.

owners: PROME/ROSTER.md (classification) · AGENTS/DAEDALUS/FLEET_MAP.tsv (maturity)

Catalyst runway

The canonical forward calendar (PROME/DOCKET.tsv) filtered to the next 21 days — scheduled events that force a test or a decision: data prints, earnings, auctions, policy meetings, gate-completion dates. Orange dates are ≤3 days out. When the regime split is heavily "Unresolved," this list is where it resolves.

Shorthand that appears on this page

HY OAS / X1high-yield credit spread (bps); >280 sustained is the pre-registered stress line ("X1"), <260 twice re-kills the axis
HOLD FLATstanding posture: no new capital deployed without a fired trigger + your approval ($500/card max-loss)
COTCFTC Commitments-of-Traders positioning data (weekly, Fri 3:30 PM)
[as-of] stampsevery number carries its observation date — a Friday vintage shown on Sunday is disclosure, not an error; refresh before acting
fire carda pre-built trade construction (TERRY) that sits shelved until its gate fires — so decision speed never requires decision haste
env / firetime chipsboot health checks: machine keys present · fire-path artifacts free of date-drift/dead pointers (known-benigns allowlisted)
spine auditweekly 8-reader (+1 anchor-leg) reconciliation of PROME's core docs against canon — the age chip shows days since last run (>7d = due)
Generated surface — points into canon, never owns it. Owner files win on any disagreement: HEARTBEAT.md (regime) · PROME/GATES.tsv (gates) · PROME/DOCKET.tsv (catalysts) · PROME/ROSTER.md + AGENTS/DAEDALUS/FLEET_MAP.tsv (fleet) · AGENTS/<NAME>/STATUS.md (agent state). Position/action views derive from FORGE and linked receipts; broker truth remains off-repo. Market levels carry their [as-of] stamps — weekend/stale vintages are shown, not hidden. Regenerate: python3 PROME/tools/fleet_dashboard.py → republish (same URL).
